## XF 2026-06-20-3 — Toxicology/pharmacology (HORMESIS: biphasic/inverted-U dose–response; Brain–Cousens 1989, Cedergreen 2005) + statistical physics & sensory neuroscience (STOCHASTIC RESONANCE): the user-mode titration fired the reopening trigger — the tail advantage is an INVERTED-U in connectivity that PEAKS at MI's users/item, which the pre-registered MONOTONE shape-menu (sharp knee / smooth saturation / flat null) cannot describe  [status: GROUNDED→PROPOSAL 2026-06-20-1]
- **⚠ Why this block exists — the ONE reopening trigger XF-20-2 named has FIRED (with the data, read-only this cycle):** XF-20-2's sole sanctioned reopening condition was, verbatim, "the user-mode titration runs and returns a result that NONE of XF-19-1…20-1 anticipated — e.g. … **non-monotone in R2**." It has now run and **is non-monotone in R2.** I recomputed `best_test.by_popularity.tail` (NDCG@10), paired text−ID, from the `results_USERTITR_*_VG.json` on disk (n_eval=94,762 and tail-bucket n=10,900 **identical at every rung** ⇒ eval held FIXED, thinning is train-only ⇒ the curve is NOT an eval-population artifact):
  - **ρ=1.00 (native VG, users/item≈3.70): tail Δ = −0.000148** (TOST-powered null, the established VG point)
  - **ρ=0.66 (users/item≈2.44 ≈ MI's 2.34): tail Δ = +0.000178 ± 0.000137, 5/5 seeds positive** ← the keystone, and it CONFIRMS the pre-registered connectivity-binding double dissociation: at the *matched* ρ=0.66 the interaction-mode tail was −0.000108 (1/5, null) while the user-mode tail is +0.000178 (5/5, crosses positive). Tail responds to R2 (connectivity), not R1 (count). **This half is anticipated — XF-19-1/-2/-3/-20-1 already frame it; it stands.**
  - **ρ=0.50 (users/item≈1.85): tail Δ = −0.000663** (1 seed, s08) — **back below zero**
  - **ρ=0.40 (users/item≈1.48): tail Δ = −0.002173** (1 seed, s08) — **strongly negative, magnitude ≈16× the keystone seed-sd**
  - ⇒ the tail advantage is an **INVERTED-U / biphasic curve that peaks essentially exactly at MI's native connectivity and then collapses (sign-flips) as users/item is thinned BELOW MI.** The head Δ stays positive and large at every rung (5/5 at ρ=0.66; +0.0049 single-seed at ρ=0.40) — only the TAIL is biphasic. **None of XF-19-1…20-1 has a frame for a non-monotone peak**: XF-19-4's pre-registered shape menu is A sharp isostatic knee / B smooth Tilman saturation / C flat null — **all three are monotone-saturating**; an inverted-U with an interior optimum and a sign flip is a *fourth* shape, and the Hill/Davies/segmented tooling (which assumes monotone curves) will mis-fit it. This is the genuine interpretive gap the reopening clause was reserved for. (Honesty caveat up front, carried below: the down-limb rungs ρ=0.50/0.40 are **single-seed**; the *peak* is 5-seed-solid, the *collapse* is suggestive-not-locked.)
- **Domain & principle:** **Hormesis** (Calabrese & Baldwin; the most-replicated dose–response form in toxicology/pharmacology) — a *biphasic* response where a stressor/agent is **stimulatory at low dose and inhibitory at high dose**, tracing an **inverted-U** (or, for a cost-metric, a J/U). It is not noise: it has its own fitted models — **Brain–Cousens (1989)**, a 5-parameter extension of the 4-param log-logistic, `f(x)=c+(d−c+f·x)/(1+exp(b·(log x−log e)))`, where the single parameter **`f` quantifies the hormetic (up-limb) effect size and `f=0` ⇒ no hormesis**, and **Cedergreen–Ritz–Streibig (2005)**, which adds `f·exp(−1/x^α)` for sharper low-dose stimulation. These give a *fittable, falsifiable* description of an inverted-U and a located optimum/zero-crossing — exactly the tooling XF-19-4 supplied for monotone curves, but for the biphasic case. The **mechanism** for *why* an interior optimum exists comes from **stochastic resonance** (statistical physics → sensory neuroscience): in a nonlinear, threshold-ish system, detection of a weak signal is **maximized at an INTERMEDIATE level of a second variable (classically noise)** — the SNR-vs-noise curve is itself an inverted-U with a resonant peak; too little and the signal stays sub-threshold, too much and it swamps the signal.
- **The analogy:** treat **connectivity (users/item) as the "dose"/"resource level"**, and the **text-stack's tail ADVANTAGE over ID (text−ID tail Δ) as the response.** The data trace a textbook biphasic curve. The stochastic-resonance/hormesis mechanism reads cleanly and **explains MI's specificity better than monotone binding does**: (i) **high connectivity** (native VG, ≈3.70): each tail ID-embedding is estimated from enough distinct users that the ID channel is already adequate → text is *redundant*, advantage ≈ 0/slightly negative (the up-limb hasn't started). (ii) **intermediate connectivity** (≈MI's 2.44): ID is under-determined (too few distinct users to pin the tail vector) **but the residual collaborative structure text must align to is still intact** → text *complements* maximally → **resonant peak** (the +0.000178 5/5 crossing). (iii) **sub-MI connectivity** (1.85→1.48): the collaborative scaffold itself is now shredded — there is too little co-occurrence signal for *either* channel, and frozen text-similarity (semantic ≠ co-purchase) actively *mis-ranks* the tail relative to the collapsed-but-unbiased ID prior → advantage **sign-flips negative** (the down-limb). The peak landing at MI's exact connectivity is then not a coincidence but the claim: **MI wins the tail because it sits near the resonant optimum of the connectivity axis, not because "less connectivity is monotonically better for text."** That is a strictly stronger, more falsifiable version of the connectivity-binding law — and it dissolves the lingering "what MI-specific property drives the tail win?" question into "MI sits at the biphasic peak."
- **Concrete model mechanism (pure-PyTorch / convergence-compliant — this is an ANALYSIS + framing reopening, NOT a new model lever):** I am explicit that under the cycle-9 freeze the implementable artifact is **(a) a read-only diagnostic fit + (b) a flagged confirmatory request to the supervisor**, *not* a new flag in `run_sasrec_sbert.py`. (a) **ANALYST, zero-GPU, today:** fit the **Brain–Cousens** (and Cedergreen) biphasic model to the `(users/item, tail Δ)` points already on disk with `scipy.optimize.curve_fit` (~15 lines: x = per-rung users/item, y = paired tail Δ, seed-bootstrap CI on the fitted optimum `x*` and the zero-crossing). Two paper numbers fall out: **the fitted peak location `x*` (testable: does `x*` ≈ MI's 2.34?)** and **`f`/the up-limb effect size with its CI (is the hormetic limb significant, or is `f`≈0 ⇒ revert to XF-19-4's monotone read?).** This *extends* XF-19-4's monotone Hill/Davies tooling to the non-monotone case — same convergence-compliant, read-only, fold-into-the-titration-figure footprint. (b) **SUPERVISOR decision (the only thing that costs GPU):** the down-limb is currently **single-seed (s08) at ρ=0.50 and ρ=0.40.** The *minimal* confirmation is **≥4 more seeds on those two ALREADY-RUN rungs** (no new lever, no new dataset, no new parameter, no new resource axis — it is *more seeds of the sanctioned user-mode titration (2)*, squarely inside envelope (2), not a forbidden "third axis"). If the supervisor judges the inverted-U worth locking before paper-freeze, this is the one cheap run; if not, the paper reports the 5-seed peak as the headline and the single-seed down-limb as a flagged, honestly-caveated observation.
- **Why no CS person would default to this:** ML/recsys reads a **non-monotone ablation sweep as noise or a bug** — "the metric went up then down, must be seed variance / a broken run" — and either drops the off-trend points or reports a monotone fit through them. **Toxicology spent 30 years establishing that biphasic/inverted-U is a real, the-single-most-common, *modelable* dose–response form (hormesis), with named 5-parameter fits and a quantified effect-size `f`** — nobody in recsys reaches for Brain–Cousens to fit an ablation curve. Likewise, **stochastic resonance** — "a weak signal is best recovered at an *intermediate* level of a second variable" — is a physics/neuroscience instinct that is *counterintuitive* to the ML default ("more data/more signal is monotonically better"); an ML engineer never hypothesizes a *resonant optimum* in dataset connectivity. The pairing (hormesis = the fittable curve form; stochastic resonance = the why) is doubly outside-CS.
- **Novelty vs our ledger:** This is the **non-monotone successor** to the monotone pre-registration. **vs XF-19-4** (Hill `n_H` / Davies-`ψ` / segmented break-point) — those tools *assume a monotone saturating curve* and would fit the up-limb while discarding or mis-locating the down-limb; Brain–Cousens/Cedergreen are the *biphasic* generalizations of exactly that toolkit. **vs XF-20-1** (Tilman ZNGI horizontal-limb = "connectivity binds the tail") — that frame predicts a *monotone* crossing into the red as R2 drops; the data instead show a crossing-then-reversal, i.e. **connectivity binds the tail with an OPTIMUM, not monotonically** — a refinement, not a contradiction (the keystone crossing it predicted still holds 5/5). **vs XF-19-2** (rigidity sharp knee) — a knee is monotone-then-plateau; this is rise-then-fall. No block in the file, and no probe in the ledger, frames a *resonant interior optimum* in a data-resource. Honest prior-art caveat: hormesis, Brain–Cousens, and stochastic resonance are **textbook — zero novelty in the ingredients**; the narrow defensible novelty is **identifying a biphasic/hormetic optimum in catalog connectivity as the mechanism of a recommender long-tail effect, and fitting it with toxicology's biphasic dose–response models.**
- **Feasibility / risk:** The diagnostic fit (a) is read-only, ~15 lines, zero-GPU, convergence-clean — do it regardless. **Primary risk = the down-limb is single-seed and there is a deflationary null I must name honestly:** at ρ=0.40/0.50 the training set is so user-thinned that *both* arms may simply be degenerating (too few interactions to learn a tail at all), making their difference a **noisy floor artifact rather than a genuine resonance** — the negative tail Δ could be "two near-broken models differ by chance," not "text mis-ranks the shredded tail." The ≥4-seed replication of those two rungs is exactly what discriminates "real hormetic down-limb (Brain–Cousens `f` sig, `x*`≈2.34, low-variance sign-consistent collapse)" from "floor noise (high-variance, sign-inconsistent across seeds)." Until that lands, the *peak* (5/5) is the publishable claim and the *inverted-U* is a flagged hypothesis. Second risk: I am reopening under a convergence freeze — mitigated by keeping the ask inside envelope (2) (more seeds of the existing run, no new axis) and routing the go/no-go to the supervisor rather than self-sanctioning. **This does NOT reopen the model-lever search** (no flag, no parameter) — it is a data-ablation shape question, like the titration itself. Sources: [Calabrese & Baldwin, *Hormesis: the dose–response revolution* (Annu. Rev. Pharmacol. Toxicol. 2003)](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12195028/); [Brain & Cousens 1989 hormesis model — drc::BC documentation](https://rdrr.io/cran/drc/man/BC.html); [Cedergreen, Ritz & Streibig 2005, *Improved empirical models describing hormesis*](https://www.researchgate.net/publication/7328938_Improved_empirical_models_describing_hormesis); [Modeling biphasic non-sigmoidal dose–response: Brain–Cousens vs Cedergreen (2023)](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/articles/PMC10462167/); [Gammaitoni et al., *Stochastic resonance* (Rev. Mod. Phys. 1998) — inverted-U SNR peak at intermediate noise](https://royalsocietypublishing.org/rspa/article/482/2333/20250945/480864/Network-stochastic-resonance-under-higher-order); [Noise improves visual motion discrimination via stochastic resonance (Front. Hum. Neurosci. 2016)](https://www.frontiersin.org/articles/10.3389/fnhum.2016.00572/full).

## XF 2026-06-23-2 — Geostatistics (ordinary kriging) ∩ quantitative genetics (GBLUP) ∩ phylogenetics (BLUP trait imputation): redundancy-DECORRELATED cold-item ID imputation — the principled successor to flat text-kNN cold-synth  [status: GROUNDED→PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-2 (2026-06-23, in-field; queued as the kriging-cold redundancy-decorrelated A/B successor to the DONE/GATE-PASSED cold-synth)]
- **Reopening trigger (why this is NO LONGER a HOLD):** the cycle-15 SUPERVISOR re-open (`AGENT_FEEDBACK §403-422`, 2026-06-23) FORMALLY LIFTED the cycle-11→14 convergence freeze and RE-OPENED the search for **cold-start-specific ideas only** ("connectivity-aware fusion variants, content→CF transfer, cold-item curricula, frozen-text neighbor borrowing"). That is the explicit "SUPERVISOR PIVOT/CONTINUE" my reopening trigger required; the 06-20→06-23 HOLD streak was correct under the now-lifted freeze. Per the directive's "ONE well-grounded idea per window … do NOT pile on more specs until the centerpiece returns a number," I queue **exactly ONE** idea and build it directly on the already-GROUNDED `cold-synth` (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1) — it is the redundancy-aware generalization of that arm, explicitly sequenced to run AFTER the 3-arm MI centerpiece + cold-synth return a number (no jump-ahead).
- **Domain & principle:** three independent fields solved the SAME problem — *predict a value at an entity with no measurement of its own, from measured entities related to it through a known similarity/covariance structure* — and all converged on the SAME estimator, the **Best Linear Unbiased Predictor (BLUP)**, which is emphatically NOT a similarity-weighted average:
  - **Geostatistics — ordinary kriging.** To estimate an unobserved location from observed ones, kriging solves `C·λ = c`, where `C` is the *data-to-data* covariance matrix (obs i vs obs j) and `c` the *data-to-target* covariance vector; weights `λ = C⁻¹c`. The decisive property (geostatisticslessons.com, "Kriging Weights in the Presence of Redundant Data"; Journel screening-effect literature): **"Where data points are clustered the weight of the cluster is divided among its members so that the individual weights are small compared with those of isolated points."** The `C⁻¹` term "considers the data redundancy between all of the data points — high covariance implies high redundancy." Inverse-distance / kNN weighting has NO `C⁻¹` and therefore **double-counts a tight cluster of near-duplicate neighbors**; kriging's covariance inverse is exactly the missing correction.
  - **Quantitative genetics — GBLUP.** To predict an un-phenotyped individual's breeding value from phenotyped relatives, GBLUP uses the *full* genomic relationship matrix `G` (not the single nearest relative) and is provably **identical to ridge-regression BLUP = kernel ridge regression** with `G` as a linear kernel (Nature *Heredity* 2021; rrBLUP). The relationship-matrix inverse decorrelates redundant relatives (full sibs, clones) for the same reason kriging declusters.
  - **Phylogenetics — BLUP trait imputation / ancestral-state reconstruction.** Imputing a missing continuous trait on an unmeasured tip under a Brownian-motion model "is mathematically equivalent to ancestral-state reconstruction and relies on the BLUP" (Rphylopars; phytools `phylo.impute`): the phylogenetic *variance matrix* (tree covariance), not the single closest relative, gives a precision-weighted prediction that down-ranks a clade of near-identical relatives.
- **The analogy:** a TRUE cold item (`item_users = 0`, the centerpiece's designed extreme) is **an unobserved location**: its learned ID embedding `item_emb[i]` never trains (noise). Its only signal is its **frozen text vector** `sbert_table[i]`. The WARM items are the "observed locations" — their ID rows DID train (real learned CF signal = the BLUP gold standard), and `sbert_table` is a similarity/covariance kernel between every cold item and every warm item AND *between warm items themselves*. The already-queued **cold-synth** imputes the cold ID row as a **flat softmax-text-kNN average of warm ID rows** — which is *exactly kriging with `C = I`*, i.e. it ASSUMES the text-neighbors are mutually independent. They are not: Amazon catalogs are dense with near-duplicate warm items (sequels, re-skins, bundle/edition variants, same-franchise SKUs). When a cold item's text-neighborhood contains a redundant cluster, flat-kNN **pulls the cold embedding toward whatever franchise-cluster is densest near it**, not toward its true position — the red-bus/blue-bus / IIA failure (XF-16-1) reappearing on the *imputation* side. Kriging/GBLUP's `C⁻¹` is the precise fix: it splits one shared vote across a redundant cluster and up-weights isolated, *informative* neighbors.
- **Concrete model mechanism (pure-PyTorch implementable here, PARAMETER-FREE):** a drop-in replacement for cold-synth's weight computation at the cold-item row-construction site (same hook cold-synth uses; flag `--krige-cold`, default OFF ⇒ cold path bit-identical to cold-synth/baseline). For each cold item `i`:
  1. **Local neighborhood ("moving-window kriging," the standard geostatistics trick to dodge the global O(n³) solve):** take its top-`m` text neighbors among WARM items only (`m≈64`), via the cosine top-k cold-synth already computes. Their frozen text vectors `T ∈ ℝ^{m×d_text}`, their trained ID rows `E_warm ∈ ℝ^{m×d}`.
  2. **Build the two kriging covariances from the FROZEN text kernel** (Gaussian or linear): data-to-data `K_ww = k(T,T) ∈ ℝ^{m×m}`, target-to-data `k_iw = k(sbert_table[i], T) ∈ ℝ^{m}`. Bandwidth = median pairwise text distance (parameter-free); fixed **nugget** `λ` on the diagonal for invertibility (standard kriging regularizer, e.g. `λ = 1e-2·tr(K_ww)/m`).
  3. **Solve the m×m local system** `w = (K_ww + λI)⁻¹ k_iw` via `torch.linalg.solve`/Cholesky — a trivial 64×64 solve per cold item, batchable over the ~15 % cold set; precompute ONCE post-train (cold rows are frozen).
  4. **Impute** `item_emb[i] ← wᵀ E_warm` (optionally renorm to the warm-row norm scale). Cold items only; warm path untouched ⇒ warm/overall metrics a guaranteed no-op (the split's required sanity check).
  **Parameter-free is the point:** every learnable cold/tail scalar in the ledger **voted itself off** (conn-gate α≈0.0011<init / W1 β=−7.42 / X1 c≈0 / Y1 T→0). cold-synth already exploits this; kriging keeps it while fixing cold-synth's independence assumption. The ONLY change vs cold-synth is replacing its `softmax(sim/τ)` weights with `(K_ww+λI)⁻¹k_iw` weights — a clean A/B that isolates the redundancy-decorrelation term.
- **Why no CS person would default to this:** ML cold-start has two reflexes — (a) **learn a content→CF mapping net** (DropoutNet, CLCRec: regress the CF embedding from content), or (b) **flat attention/kNN over content similarity** (= cold-synth). Nobody treats the trained ID-embedding *table* as a **spatial random field** and *kriges* the missing rows with a redundancy-decorrelating BLUP, because that is geostatistics / animal-breeding / phylogenetics, not representation learning. The GP-regression view exists in ML, but this specific realization — *local moving-window kriging over a FROZEN-text kernel, parameter-free, whose entire contribution is the `C⁻¹` declustering flat content-kNN structurally cannot do* — is imported wholesale from three non-CS fields that each independently found BLUP beats nearest-relative averaging. A recsys engineer reaches for an attention head or a learned projection, never "your cold-item content neighbors are clustered, so solve the kriging system instead of averaging them."
- **Novelty vs ledger:** nearest probe = **cold-synth (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1)** = flat softmax-text-kNN over warm ID rows = *literally the `C = I` (independent-neighbor) special case of this estimator*. This restores the data-to-data covariance inverse cold-synth drops — predicted to DIVERGE from cold-synth exactly for cold items whose text-neighbors are internally redundant (dense franchise clusters) and to win there. Distinct from **conn-gate** (DEAD warm-tail: a learnable fusion *gate* at the score — this is parameter-free *imputation* of the representation). Distinct from **XF-17-4 BBP / Gavish–Donoho spectral shrinkage** (GROUNDED): that *denoises the WARM matrix that already trained*; this *imputes COLD rows that never trained* — opposite operands. Distinct from TAPE/text-prototypes (always-on additive capacity for all items; this is a one-shot cold-only BLUP). No ledger probe inverts a neighbor-neighbor covariance to decluster the cold imputation; genuinely new.
- **Feasibility / risk:** default-OFF flag, parameter-free (no scalar to vote off; the no-op IS the flag being off), leak-free by construction (warm ID rows = train-only; cold neighbors + kernel = frozen text; cold row never enters training; cold items remain valid/test TARGETS only — the guards cold-synth already passed). Cost: one `m×m` Cholesky per cold item, precomputed once over ~15 % of items ⇒ seconds. **Main failure mode:** if cold-item text-neighborhoods are NOT internally redundant here, `K_ww≈I` and kriging collapses back to cold-synth (a clean *neutral* vs cold-synth, not a regression — still a publishable "decorrelation buys nothing; neighborhoods already diverse" finding). **Second risk:** ill-conditioned `K_ww` if duplicate-text neighbors collide — the fixed `λI` nugget prevents it; log the median condition number as an interpretable readout. **Honesty guard (carry the conn-gate lesson):** report cold NDCG **absolute + rel % + cold hit-count + warm-no-op sanity**, paired vs cold-synth AND text-stack, ≥5-seed, 95 % CI; NO single-seed rel % claim. **Sequencing (per cycle-15 "don't pile on"):** queue this as the cold-synth SUCCESSOR — in-field should HOLD it until the 3-arm MI centerpiece + cold-synth return a number, then ground it as the redundancy-decorrelated A/B against cold-synth (one extra arm, same split/seed), NOT before.

  - **Astronomy — Malmquist/selection-function norm calibration** (rescale imputed cold-embedding norm to the warm-row norm distribution so cold items compete on direction, not magnitude). The underlying pathology is real (dot-product retrieval up-ranks high-norm items; cold/imputed rows are under-normed), **but it is already a 2025 RecSys paper** — Meehan et al., *"On Inherited Popularity Bias in Cold-Start Item Recommendation"* (arXiv:2510.11402), which explicitly proposes *"scaling the magnitudes of output item representations."* A recsys engineer DOES default to norm-debiasing now ⇒ the Malmquist dressing adds no new mechanism. (Kriging already offers the optional warm-norm renorm as an aside; if a norm A/B is wanted it belongs folded into the kriging arm, not as a cross-field "novelty.")
  - **Optimal transport / Sinkhorn barycentric content→CF map** (one global entropic-OT plan from the warm text-distribution to the warm CF-distribution; map cold text → CF via the barycentric projection). Mechanistically clean, but **OT-for-cold-start CF has direct prior art** — *Wasserstein Collaborative Filtering for Item Cold-start* (arXiv:1909.04266), SinkhornCF (WWW'21), DUP-OT cross-domain (arXiv:2508.16210) — AND it lands in the SAME impute-the-cold-row family as the already-stronger-novelty kriging (which has NO recsys cold-start precedent). Lower novelty, redundant axis ⇒ do not spend a slot here.
- **TEED UP for the NEXT window (after kriging returns a number — NOT grounded now, to respect the rate-limit).** If the impute-the-cold-row family (cold-synth → kriging) tops out near the current floor (≈8–30 hits/8–14k), the residual gap is no longer *which* warm rows to average but *why a text-kNN average of warm CF rows barely transfers at all* — i.e. the **text→CF alignment quality** itself. The genuinely-distinct, not-obviously-CS surfaces to mine THEN (one, after resolution): (a) a closed-form **morphometric/geodetic registration** (orthogonal Procrustes / Helmert datum-transform from the warm frozen-text subspace onto the warm learned-CF subspace, applied to cold text vectors) — note the honest dent: NLP cross-lingual alignment uses Procrustes, so its novelty is "recsys-cold-start hasn't, NLP has"; (b) **cold-item curricula** (the one re-opened surface no arm has touched). I am NOT grounding either now; they are pre-flagged so the slot isn't wasted re-deriving the prior-art levers above.
- **Reopening / next-idea condition:** kriging (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-2) returns a 5-seed number, OR the 3-arm centerpiece goes to 5 seeds and the imputation family is confirmed near-floor ⇒ next cross-field window grounds ONE residual-gap idea (from the teed-up surfaces, vetted against prior art). - **FRESH PRIOR-ART READ (the cycle's one new reconnaissance result) — econometric attenuation-correction is the THEORY for norm-restore, not an independent 3rd lever.** The @50–100-rank ramp under a literal-zero @10 (XF-23-3) reads as a *systematic down-ranking of the imputed cold row* — the classic **errors-in-variables / regression-dilution** signature (Spearman 1904, "correction for attenuation"; regression-dilution attenuation bias): a predictor measured/imputed with error has its effective coefficient **attenuated toward zero** by the reliability ratio `λ = σ²_signal/(σ²_signal+σ²_error)`, and the textbook fix is to **inflate it by `1/λ`** to undo the dilution. Mapped here: a synthesized cold ID row is a high-variance point estimate, so its dot-product contribution is diluted vs trained warm rows ⇒ it ranks just under threshold. The correction = a **per-item reliability-weighted magnitude inflation**. **But that is exactly what norm-restore (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-3) already attacks** — attenuation-correction supplies the *principled weighting* (item-specific `1/λ` from imputation variance) over a uniform norm rescale, so it FOLDS INTO norm-restore as a refinement, **it is NOT a separable 3rd spec.** And the generic "uncertainty-aware cold embedding" dressing is already **2023–2025 CS prior art** (Uncertainty-aware Consistency Learning, arXiv:2308.03470; Embedding-uncertainty cold-start, ScienceDirect S0925231225028164) ⇒ fails the no-CS-default bar. Net: the attenuation framing *reinforces* "let norm-restore resolve," it does not justify a new block.
- **SELF-CORRECTION — fold cycle-16 FIX #1 into my own kriging block (XF-23-2) sanity check.** Cycle-16's AUDIT CATCH proved the "warm-no-op / warm bit-identical" claim **empirically FALSE**: overwriting *cold* rows still raises *warm* + overall NDCG (+0.0003–0.0008, 5/5, both datasets) because the cold rows are full-catalog **competitors** — changing them changes warm items' ranks through the shared softmax denominator (still leak-free). My XF-23-2 step-4 assertion *"warm path untouched ⇒ warm/overall metrics a guaranteed no-op (the split's required sanity check)"* is the SAME false claim: kriging also only overwrites cold rows, so it too will shift warm via the competitor set. **Correction (for in-field/EXPERIMENT to apply when grounding/running kriging AND norm-restore):** the smoke-test/sanity check must read **"warm within ±0.001 (small competitor-set effect, NOT bit-identical)"**, not "guaranteed no-op" — identical to the FIX #1 rewrite cycle-16 mandated for the queued proposals. The leak-free guarantee is unaffected (cold rows never enter training); only the "no-op" language is wrong.
- **Reopening / next-idea condition (unchanged, re-affirmed):** a cold spec returns a 5-seed number (norm-restore OR kriging), OR the imputation family is confirmed near-floor at 5 seeds ⇒ next window grounds ONE residual-gap idea from the XF-23-3 teed-up surfaces (Procrustes/Helmert text→CF registration; cold-item curricula), vetted against prior art. The s08 result is the **opposite**: inflating the magnitude of a directionally-imperfect averaged vector **amplified its directional error** and pushed the true next item further down the ranking (dot-product retrieval rewards high-norm rows, so a big vector pointed slightly wrong out-ranks a small vector pointed slightly wrong for MANY wrong targets). If this holds at 5 seeds, it **refutes the magnitude hypothesis** and re-locates the residual gap in the **DIRECTION/alignment quality** of the imputed row — i.e. the flat text-kNN average lands in roughly-right-but-not-right *bearing* within the CF space, and no amount of magnitude surgery fixes a bearing error. **This is exactly the surface the teed-up Procrustes/Helmert text→CF registration attacks** (it corrects the *rotation/reflection* mapping frozen-text geometry onto learned-CF geometry, leaving magnitude to the warm-norm renorm as a separable knob). - **PRE-REGISTERED IDEA KEPT READY (not grounded — respects the rate-limit + NO-PIVOT ruling).** The Procrustes/Helmert **closed-form, parameter-free** registration of the warm frozen-text subspace onto the warm learned-CF subspace (prior-art-vetted in XF-23-4: no recsys-cold-start precedent for a *closed-form orthogonal/similarity* content→CF map; all literature maps are *learned* nets ⇒ genuine no-CS-default edge) remains the **default next grounding**. Its mechanism, restated tightly so in-field can ground it in one pass the instant the trigger fires: solve `R* = argmin_R ‖ T_warm R − E_warm ‖_F` s.t. `RᵀR=I` over the WARM items (T_warm = frozen text vecs, E_warm = trained ID rows) via SVD of `T_warmᵀ E_warm = UΣVᵀ ⇒ R* = UVᵀ` (add an optional isotropic scale `s = tr(Σ)/‖T_warm‖_F²` for the Helmert/similarity variant); then impute each COLD row as `item_emb[i] ← s · sbert_table[i] · R*` (optionally warm-norm-renorm), a **one-shot, cold-only, closed-form** operation — no learnable scalar to vote itself off, echoing why cold-synth/kriging beat W1/X1/Y1/conn-gate. Flag `--procrustes-cold`, default OFF ⇒ cold path bit-identical; warm within ±0.001 (competitor-set shift, NOT bit-identical — the cycle-16 FIX #1 language, applies here too). It is DISTINCT from norm-restore (which fixes magnitude and just failed on s08) precisely because it fixes **direction** — the axis the s08 number now implicates.
- **Reopening / next-idea condition (tightened by this cycle's number):** ground ONE residual-gap idea — **default = Procrustes/Helmert text→CF registration** — when ANY of: (i) kriging (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-2) returns a 5-seed number; OR (ii) norm-restore's regression **confirms at ≥3 seeds** (⇒ magnitude-fix refuted ⇒ direction is the live axis ⇒ Procrustes is the natural successor, sequenced AFTER kriging so the GPU isn't contended); OR (iii) a supervisor cycle-18 explicitly re-points cross-field. ## XF 2026-07-03-1 — Actuarial credibility theory (Bühlmann Z=n/(n+k)) ∩ psychometric test-equating (equipercentile / IRT common-item linking): a SCORE-level, per-item credibility-routed, scale-EQUATED merge of a warm CF space and a homogeneous frozen-text space — the fix the cold_id_drop(0)-vs-sbert-only(66) double-dissociation demands, that every representation-level gate structurally could not deliver  [status: GROUNDED→PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-1 (2026-07-03, in-field; grounded as the score-level dual-space credibility-routed merge — verified feasible/leak-free/novel vs `--zfusion-eval` + conn-gate/CF1/X1 + the imputation family; headline = TRAIN-CONSISTENT variant per ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-2 Finding 2, with the eval-only merge as the cheap first probe)]
- **⚠ STEP 0 / STEP 1 + REOPENING TRIGGER (this is NOT a HOLD — my pre-registered condition FIRED).** STEP 0: `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` re-verified ABSENT on disk (2026-07-03) ⇒ no termination. STEP 1: governing order unchanged = ORCHESTRATION STATUS BOARD **cycle-17** + AGENT_FEEDBACK **cycle-17** (no cycle-18 yet). My XF-2026-07-02-2 reopening condition was *"ground ONE residual-gap idea when kriging returns a number OR the imputation family is confirmed near-floor."* **BOTH now hold on disk (JSON 457→472 since 07-02):** kriging RAN (`results_KRIGE_cold_MI_seed20260608.json`), norm-restore stands (regression), and the sanctioned `--sbert-only` diagnostic RAN at **5 seeds on BOTH datasets** (`results_COLD_sbertonly_hstu_{MI,VG}_seed2026060{8..12}.json`) + a new `COLDIDDROP` arm. The imputation family (cold-synth/kriging/norm) is **confirmed near-floor**, and — the surprise — the "diagnostic" `--sbert-only` is the **strongest cold arm by ~8–9×**. This is exactly the "impute-the-row family tops out; the residual gap is the text→CF *space*, not the row" scenario I teed up in XF-23-3 — but the resolution is cleaner and re-points the idea. I ground exactly ONE idea (rate-limit honored), and it SUPERSEDES the teed-up Procrustes/Helmert default (which is still a *row-level* fix, and the data below shows row-level fixes are dominated). NOTE the honesty flag for SUPERVISOR: cycle-17's NO-PIVOT ruling ("reconsider only if sbert-only + norm-restore + kriging ALL resolve near-floor") is **not** met — sbert-only did the OPPOSITE of resolving near-floor (it WON cold ~9×), so the correct reading is *escalate/understand this positive*, not pivot.
- **THE DECISIVE NEW DATA (raw cross-field reconnaissance; ANALYST owns the formal seed-paired verdict). MI, `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, 20-ep, full eval, `by_coldstart`:**
  - **A clean 5-seed DOUBLE DISSOCIATION (NDCG@10):** full-stack (ID+text+TAPE, = cold-synth run) overall **0.0370** / warm **0.0437** / cold **0.00038**; `--sbert-only` (homogeneous text space, ALL items scored by `sbert_proj` only) overall **0.0143** / warm **0.0163** / cold **0.00300**. ⇒ the learned-ID table is *essential* for warm (full-stack 2.7× on warm) but is *pure noise that corrupts* cold (sbert-only ~8× on cold; VG: cold 0.00506 vs cold-synth 0.00084, ~6×, 5-seed both datasets, hit@10 66/8748 MI, 156/14132 VG). Neither model is globally best; the two channels' reliabilities **cross over** with observation count.
  - **The row-level-vs-space-level discriminator (single-seed s08, decisive on direction):** `cold_id_drop` — which ZEROES the cold item's noise ID row but leaves it in the full space (`e = 0 + sbert_proj + m`, scored against warm competitors that keep their high-norm trained ID rows) — gets **0 hit@10**. `--sbert-only` — which removes the ID row from **every** item, cold AND warm, so they compete in one homogeneous space — gets **66 hit@10**. kriging (row-level impute) = **1 hit**; norm-restore (row-level magnitude) = **0**. **Fixing the cold item's own representation is near-floor; homogenizing the SPACE both items are ranked in is the ~9× win.**
- **Domain & principle (two non-CS fields that each solved "combine two differently-scaled estimates, weighting by how much data backs each"):**
  - **Actuarial credibility theory — Bühlmann (1967).** To set a policyholder's premium the actuary blends the *individual's own* loss experience `X̄` (credible only when the insured has a long history) with the *collective/portfolio* mean `μ` (the reliable fallback when the individual's data is thin): `P = Z·X̄ + (1−Z)·μ`, with the **credibility factor `Z = n/(n+k)`**, `k = EPV/VHM` (process-variance / hypothetical-means-variance). `Z→0` at `n=0` (trust the collective entirely) and `Z→1` as experience accrues. It is the exact, closed-form, **non-learnable** "how much do I trust this entity's own signal vs a shared prior, as a function of its observation count."
  - **Psychometric test-equating — equipercentile / IRT common-item linking (Spearman & Brown 1910 lineage; Kolen & Brennan).** Scores from two different test *forms* (different items, different difficulty/scale) are NOT directly comparable; before you can rank examinees who took different forms on one list, you must **equate** — place both score distributions on a common metric (equipercentile: match by percentile; IRT: a linear transform onto a common θ-scale via anchor items). The lesson: *two measurements on different instruments cannot be merged into one ranking until their scales are equated.*
- **The analogy:** our two channels ARE two "test forms" measuring the same next-item relevance on **different scales**: the CF/ID space (full-stack warm scores live at ~0.044-magnitude, dot-products against trained high-norm rows) and the homogeneous frozen-text space (sbert-only scores live at ~0.016-magnitude). An item's **train interaction count `n_i` is its "experience"**: warm items have credible ID experience (`Z_i→1`, rank them in the CF space), cold items have none (`Z_i→0`, the frozen-text space is their only credible signal — and the double-dissociation proves it's ~9× better for them). The reason cold_id_drop fails and sbert-only wins is a **scale/equating failure**: a cold item scored in the CF space (even with its own row zeroed) is out-competed by warm items' high-norm ID rows in the shared softmax denominator — the two "forms" were merged into one ranking *without equating*. Bühlmann says *how to weight* (Z=n/(n+k)); test-equating says *you must put the two score channels on a common scale first*. Together they prescribe the model neither full-stack nor sbert-only is: **rank warm items by their CF score and cold items by their (scale-equated) text score, in one merged list.**
- **Concrete model mechanism (pure-PyTorch implementable here; NON-learnable; flag `--credibility-route`, default OFF ⇒ current path bit-identical):** a SCORE-level (not representation-level) router at eval + in the training softmax. For user-state `h` and every candidate `j`, compute **two catalog score vectors** (both already constructible from existing code):
  1. `s_full = h @ all_item_features().T` — the current CF space (`item_emb + sbert_proj + m`), the code's existing scoring path.
  2. `s_txt  = h @ Ptext.T`, where `Ptext = sbert_proj(sbert_table(all))` — the homogeneous frozen-text space (exactly the `sbert_only` branch, `item_features` L751-753), computed once per step as a second projection of the SAME `h` (no second encoder — `h` is shared; only the item-feature table differs).
  3. **Equate the two score scales (test-equating step, per-query, parameter-free):** standardize each vector over the catalog, `z_full = (s_full − μ_full)/σ_full`, `z_txt = (s_txt − μ_txt)/σ_txt` (mean/std over the `n_items` candidates for THIS query) — the CTT/linear-equating analogue of equipercentile matching; both channels now on a common z-metric so they co-rank.
  4. **Credibility-route per candidate (Bühlmann step):** `Z_j = n_j/(n_j + k)` from the train-only frequency buffer `item_freq` (already registered; leak-free), `k` a **single non-learnable hyperparameter** (swept `{0.5,1,2,5}`, or fixed by matching the sbert-only cold endpoint). Final score `s_j = Z_j·z_full_j + (1−Z_j)·z_txt_j`. At `n_j=0` (true cold) ⇒ `Z_j=0` ⇒ candidate ranked purely by the equated text score = **exactly the sbert-only regime that wins cold ~9×**; at large `n_j` ⇒ `Z_j→1` ⇒ CF space = **the full-stack regime that wins warm**. The crossover is placed by `k`, not spread across the whole tail. **Non-learnable is the point** — every learnable cold/tail scalar in the ledger voted itself off (conn-gate α≈0.0011, X1 c≈0, Y1 T→0, W1 β=−7.42); Z=n/(n+k) and the z-equating have no gradient-trained scalar to collapse, mirroring why the non-learnable cold-synth/sbert-only are the only arms that work.
  - **Attachment:** the merge lands wherever full-catalog scores are formed — eval ranking (`forward`/eval scorer, ~L693) and, to keep train/eval consistent, the `chunked_full_softmax_loss` scoring (~L698-764, add the second `z_txt` accumulation alongside the existing running-logit loop; the per-chunk `μ,σ` need one extra pass or a two-moment accumulator — same incremental pattern as the current LSE loop). **Simplest first probe (eval-only, cheapest):** apply the router at EVAL scoring only (leave training loss untouched) — a pure inference-time re-rank of the already-trained full-stack model's two score vectors; if the eval-only merge lifts cold without denting warm, promote to the train-consistent version.
- **Why no CS person would default to this:** the entire CS cold-start-fusion literature merges channels at the **representation level, in ONE space, with a LEARNED module** — MWUF meta scaling/shifting (arXiv:2105.04790) *transforms* cold ID embeddings toward warm space via a trained net; adversarial-VAE warm-up (arXiv:2302.14395) *generates* warm-aligned embeddings; gated-fusion KG models learn a dynamic gate. Nobody computes **two scores in two different spaces and psychometrically EQUATES them** before merging, and nobody sets the per-item mix with an **actuarial credibility factor `Z=n/(n+k)`** — that is insurance experience-rating + educational-measurement test-equating, not representation learning. A recsys engineer reaches for a learned gate or a meta-net (both tried here → dead); the cross-field move is "these are two test forms on different scales; equate them and weight by credibility."
- **Novelty vs ledger:** nearest probes are the DEAD representation-level single-space blends — **CF1 cue-fusion** (`w_id·e + w_tx·sbert_proj`, inverse-variance precision, sum-2 mass; DEAD both datasets, L829-836), **conn-gate** (learnable α on log-users, DEAD, L819-828), **Z1 fitness-gate** (non-learnable ID-damping by log-freq sigmoid, DEAD/tail −75%, L810-813), **X1** (learnable shrink of ID toward the TAPE prototype `m`, DEAD, L803-806). **ALL FOUR combine the channels inside ONE score/space at the representation level** — and the `cold_id_drop`(0)-vs-`sbert-only`(66) contrast is direct evidence that class *structurally cannot* win, because it never equates the two scales or lets cold items escape the CF-space denominator dominated by warm high-norm rows. This is the FIRST **score-level, dual-space, scale-equated** router: two scores, two spaces, an explicit per-query equating transform, and a non-learnable count-credibility weight. Also DISTINCT from the row-level imputation family (cold-synth/kriging/norm/Procrustes) — those all rewrite the cold *row* inside the CF space (near-floor); this leaves rows alone and fixes the *ranking space*. Critically, **Z1/conn-gate/CF1's death does NOT bind here:** they were tuned/killed on the WARM tail (n_i small but >0, ID row has real signal) *before* the cold split existed; the sbert-only 5-seed double-dissociation is fresh evidence that at the TRUE-COLD endpoint (n_i=0) the text-only regime — this router's `Z→0` limit — wins by ~9×.
- **Feasibility / risk:** default-OFF flag ⇒ current scoring bit-identical; non-learnable (only `k`, a swept scalar — the no-op IS the flag off, as with cold-synth/kriging); leak-free (`n_j` train-only `item_freq`; text frozen; no label/target consulted). Cost: one extra catalog scoring (`h @ Ptext.T`) + two moments per query — cheap (a second matmul; `Ptext` cacheable). **Main risk = it touches EVAL scoring** (the supervisor's standing caution): mitigated because it injects NO popularity term and consults NO label — and it *down-weights* the ID channel for LOW-count items (`Z_j` small), the OPPOSITE of head-inflation, so it cannot exploit the popularity-hit reward that trap warns about (if anything it sacrifices some head precision — report warm/overall as the sanity guard). **Second risk = the z-equating is only a linear (CTT) equate; if the two score distributions are non-linearly mis-scaled, cold gains could cost warm** — guard by the eval-only first probe (cheapest, fully reversible) and, if linear equating is insufficient, upgrade to per-query **rank/quantile** equating (the equipercentile analogue). **Honesty guards (carry the conn-gate s08-mirage + cycle-16 FIX #1 lessons):** report cold **absolute + rel% + hit-count + warm/overall no-regression**, ≥5-seed, 95% CI, paired vs BOTH full-stack and sbert-only; NO single-seed rel% claim; the warm sanity is "within noise of full-stack," and note the merge shifts warm ranks via the shared list (competitor-set effect, NOT bit-identical — cycle-16 FIX #1 language). **Predicted outcome map (pre-registered):** best case = a single model that keeps full-stack's warm (~0.044) AND captures sbert-only's cold (~0.0030) — a genuine "no cold/warm trade-off" result; null case = the linear equate can't reconcile the scales and it lands between the two endpoints (still a clean, publishable characterization of the channel-reliability crossover + the double-dissociation figure). Either way the **double-dissociation itself is the paper-grade cold-start finding** (channel reliability crosses over with observation count; the optimal recommender routes per-item and must scale-equate to co-rank) — this router is the constructive test of whether that crossover is *exploitable* in one model.
- **Reopening / next-idea condition:** this idea returns a ≥5-seed number (eval-only probe first, then train-consistent), OR a supervisor cycle-18 re-points cross-field ⇒ next window grounds ONE residual-gap idea — if the linear z-equate underperforms, the pre-vetted successor is **equipercentile/rank equating** (the non-linear psychometric upgrade, same dual-space frame); the teed-up Procrustes/Helmert text→CF registration drops to reserve (row-level, dominated by the space-level result above); cold-item curricula stays reserve #2. Until then: this is the single grounded idea for the window. Sources read this cycle (read-only, no external posting): Inverse-variance weighting (en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inverse-variance_weighting); Bühlmann credibility Z=n/(n+k) (en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Credibility_theory; openacttexts.github.io Loss-Data-Analytics Ch.9; SOA STAM notes); Spearman–Brown prophecy formula (en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spearman–Brown_prediction_formula); test equating / equipercentile / IRT common-item linking (en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Equating; ETS Livingston "Equating Test Scores Without IRT"; assess.com/test-equating); CS prior-art vetted-against: MWUF meta scaling/shifting (arXiv:2105.04790), adversarial-VAE cold-start warm-up (arXiv:2302.14395). [status: HOLD — queues NOTHING (no new spec, no run, no lever, no dataset, no GPU, no new params); a number-bearing terminal trigger-check + mechanism post-mortem, not a groundable idea]

The reopening trigger is now 'the credibility-router resolves (win or refute).' If it REFUTES the deployable gate, the branch is CLOSED and NO new cold lever may be grounded without a fresh SUPERVISOR directive."* My reopening trigger has **fired** (the router resolved), but the resolution is a **REFUTE**, whose codified consequence is *branch CLOSED / no new cold lever*. So this window is a HOLD that queues nothing and instead resolves the trigger honestly on-disk.
- **TRIGGER CHECK — FIRED → REFUTE (the cycle's decisive intelligence; ANALYST owns the formal seed-paired verdict, this is single-seed as pre-registered).** The credibility-router (`--credibility-route` eval-only + `--cred-route-train`) is **implemented and RAN** (`run_sasrec_sbert.py` now carries the flags; `results_SMOKE_credroute_MI` + `results_SMOKE_credtrain_MI` passed no-NaN; JSON count 474→481). MI, `--encoder hstu --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, 20-ep, `by_coldstart`, seed 20260608 — encoder-matched to `COLDSYNTH_MI_s08` / `COLD_sbertonly_hstu_MI_s08` (Finding-3 confound avoided):

  | arm (MI s08, HSTU) | cold hit@10 | cold N@10 | warm N@10 | overall N@10 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | cold-synth (warm-preserving CEILING) | **7** | 0.000342 | 0.04379 | 0.03717 |
  | sbert-only (cold WINNER / warm-destroyer) | **66** | 0.003108 | 0.01632 | 0.01431 |
  | **CREDROUTE eval-only** | **3** | 0.000114 | 0.04334 | 0.03675 |
  | **CREDROUTE train-consistent** | **3** | 0.000104 | 0.04369 | 0.03705 |

  **Pre-registered DEPLOYABLE GATE (cycle-18, BOTH must hold): (i) cold hit@10 > 7; (ii) warm N@10 ≥ ~0.040.** BOTH router variants **FAIL (i)** — cold hit@10 = **3**, *below* even the cold-synth ceiling of 7 and ≪ sbert-only's 66 — while **passing (ii)** (warm preserved at ~0.0437). It is the textbook **"warm-preserving hybrid floors cold"** signature the 2-dataset/5-seed double-dissociation predicted: routing per-item count into a z-equated text channel keeps warm (Z→1 ⇒ ≈pure CF) but does NOT recover sbert-only's cold win (Z→0 limit lands at 3 hits, not 66). Honesty guards honored: absolute + hit-count reported, NO single-seed rel% claim, no zero-comparator t-stat. **The router is REFUTED on both variants at single-seed ⇒ no 5-seed escalation (correct per the gate); the cold-start branch's one remaining arm is spent.**
- **CROSS-FIELD POST-MORTEM — WHY the Bühlmann/test-equating router could not exploit the double-dissociation (mechanism, honestly hedged single-seed).** The router's premise (XF-2026-07-03-1) was that our two channels are two *test forms* on different scales, so z-equating + a count-credibility weight `Z=n/(n+k)` would let cold items be ranked in the text form (where sbert-only scores 66) and warm in the CF form. The number says the premise's hidden assumption is false **here**: the frozen-text channel `s_txt = h @ Ptext.T` is a **trained RESIDUAL head** — `sbert_proj` is optimized end-to-end as an *additive correction on top of* the learned ID row, never as a standalone ranking metric. So at eval the "second test form" the equating step assumes to exist **does not exist**: stripping the ID row and z-standardizing `sbert_proj` does not conjure sbert-only's well-calibrated cold ordering, because sbert-only only *earns* that ordering by being trained as the SOLE channel (its `sbert_proj` learns a full metric, not a residual). Test-equating can reconcile two *real* scales; it cannot manufacture a calibrated scale that was never trained. This is the constructive-side confirmation of the ANALYST's cycle-17/18 conclusion — *cold lift requires text-PRIMARY training, which sacrifices warm* — and it explains why the double-dissociation is a genuine **structural tension**, not an engineering gap a clever eval-time or count-routed merge can close in a pure sequential stack. (This is analysis of an already-run arm, NOT a new lever.)
- **CONSEQUENCE per cycle-18 (codified, not my discretion).** Cycle-18: *"If cold floors OR warm collapses ⇒ REFUTED (single-seed, no 5-seed) ⇒ the cold-start branch is DEFINITIVELY CLOSED with an even STRONGER impossibility result… Either outcome is publishable and closes the branch,"* and *"Re-open the pivot question ONLY if the credibility-router (both variants) also fails the deployable gate — at which point the cold-start branch is DEFINITIVELY complete… and the program reverts, terminally, to PAPER-FINISHING."* **Both variants failed ⇒ the terminal condition is MET.** The cold-start experimental program is COMPLETE; the deliverable is the paper(s) + the resolved cold-start study, whose §5.5 finding is now the full **impossibility boundary**: (a) additive text = 0 on cold (MI & VG); (b) pure text-primary ranks cold (MI 64.8±1.9 / VG 153.0±7.3 hits@10, 5/5) but sacrifices ~60–70% warm; (c) EVERY warm-preserving hybrid — additive / cold-id-drop / cold-synth / kriging / norm-restore / z-fusion proxy / **and now the parameter-free credibility-router** — floors cold (cold-synth the 7-hit ceiling) ⇒ *cold-lift and warm-CF are in structural tension in a pure sequential stack; a co-trained architecture is required to approach the ~0.057 incumbent.* The router being **parameter-free** makes this the strongest possible negative: even the analyst's prescribed joint-routing, realized without a single learnable scalar to vote itself off, cannot jointly rank cold and preserve warm.
- **WHAT I GROUND THIS WINDOW: NOTHING (correct + directive-compliant).** The reopening consequence for cross-field is explicit — *branch CLOSED, no new cold lever without a fresh supervisor directive.* The row-level imputation family is EXHAUSTED/FORBIDDEN (cold-synth robust local optimum; kriging/norm/id-drop REFUTED); the score-level dual-space router (the one novel escalation) is now REFUTED; the Procrustes/Helmert row-level idea is SUBSUMED/retired (dominated by the space-level result, and row-level fixes floor). There is no admissible cross-field surface left inside the sanctioned scope, and the DEAD model-lever list + closed new-lever search bar any *overall*-track analogy too. Grounding anything now would violate the CONVERGENCE RULE that this pipeline's discipline depends on. **HOLD is the honest, maximal-value act.**

## XF 2026-07-03-3 — RATE-LIMITED HOLD (branch terminally CLOSED; queue NOTHING runnable) + a SHARPENED co-training RESERVE: veterinary/medical epidemiology (Hui–Walter two-imperfect-tests latent-class model; conditional-independence error-decorrelation) as the concrete answer to the post-mortem's "the text channel is a trained RESIDUAL, not a standalone test-form" diagnosis  [status: HOLD — queues NOTHING (no new spec, no run, no lever, no dataset, no GPU, no new params); a trigger-recheck + one deepened, prior-art-vetted RESERVE mechanism for the SINGLE door the impossibility boundary left open, kept explicitly UN-grounded pending a fresh supervisor directive]
- **⚠ STEP 0 / STEP 1 + TRIGGER STATE — still CLOSED, no reopening condition has fired.** STEP 0: `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` re-verified ABSENT on disk (2026-07-03) ⇒ no termination (overall 0.0673 ≪ hardware-blocked 0.0760; best deployable true-cold arm ~0.0004 ≪ LC2C ~0.057). STEP 1: governing order unchanged = ORCHESTRATION STATUS BOARD **cycle-18** + AGENT_FEEDBACK **cycle-18** (2026-07-03; newest — **no cycle-19 written yet**; `grep '^## FEEDBACK' AGENT_FEEDBACK.md` tops out at cycle-18). Cycle-18's tightened CONVERGENCE RULE: *"the program is BOUNDED to ONE remaining arm — the credibility-router … If it REFUTES the deployable gate, the branch is CLOSED and NO new cold lever may be grounded without a fresh SUPERVISOR directive."* That arm RAN and **REFUTED both variants** (XF 2026-07-03-2: eval-only + train-consistent both cold hit@10 = 3 < the cold-synth ceiling of 7; warm preserved ~0.0437). Disk since then: JSON **474 → 494** — the new files are honest **encoder-matched KRIGE re-runs** (`results_KRIGE_cold_hstu_{MI,VG}_seed2026060{8..12}.json`, 5-seed both datasets on **HSTU**) + `V2_confirm` overall-paper seeds; the KRIGE-on-HSTU set closes the ANALYST Finding-3 encoder confound and — as expected — **corroborates the closure** (kriging floors cold on the matched encoder too), it does NOT reopen anything. `Get-Process python` = NONE, GPU idle. **No cycle-19, no PIVOT, no human re-point ⇒ my reopening condition (XF 2026-07-03-2: "a supervisor cycle-19 or the human re-points cross-field OR sanctions a genuine PIVOT") is UNMET. HOLD is the directive-compliant act; grounding a runnable lever now would breach the CONVERGENCE RULE this pipeline's discipline depends on.**
- **WHY THIS IS NOT AN EMPTY HOLD — I am deepening the ONE surface the impossibility boundary explicitly leaves open.** The cold-start finding is now a proven **structural tension**: cold-lift needs text-PRIMARY training (sbert-only wins cold ~8–9×) but that sacrifices ~60–70% warm CF; EVERY warm-preserving bolt-on (additive / cold-id-drop / cold-synth / kriging / norm-restore / z-fusion proxy / the parameter-free credibility-router) floors cold. Cycle-18 §5.5 states the escape hatch verbatim: *"a co-trained architecture is required to reach the ~0.057 incumbent."* The credibility-router POST-MORTEM (XF 2026-07-03-2) pinned the precise mechanism of failure: `s_txt = h @ Ptext.T` is a **trained RESIDUAL head** — `sbert_proj` is optimized only as an *additive correction on top of the ID row*, never as a standalone ranking metric, so at eval there is **no second "test form" to equate**; test-equating can reconcile two real scales but cannot manufacture a calibrated one that was never trained. Any future reopening therefore hinges on ONE question: *how do you co-train the text channel so it is a genuinely standalone-calibrated relevance metric (usable ALONE for cold items) WITHOUT collapsing warm CF?* This window I bring the outside-CS mechanism that answers exactly that — kept as a RESERVE, not grounded.
- **Domain & principle (genuinely outside CS — veterinary / medical diagnostic epidemiology):** the **Hui–Walter latent-class model** (Hui & Walter 1980) estimates the accuracy of **two imperfect diagnostic tests with NO gold standard**, by assuming the two tests are **conditionally independent given the true (latent) disease status** — i.e. their *errors do not correlate once you condition on the truth*. That single assumption is what makes each test *identifiable as a standalone instrument* rather than one being a mere echo/residual of the other: if two tests err together, they carry one signal; if their errors are conditionally independent, each is an *independently sufficient* readout of the latent variable, and their agreement/disagreement is informative. (Confirmed current-standard: it is THE reference method when no gold standard exists; conditional-dependence extensions exist precisely because violating that independence destroys standalone identifiability — nature.com/articles/srep11861, woah.org Bayesian-LCM notes.) The complementary "make each readout standalone" training device on the CS side is **deep supervision** (each branch/exit carries its OWN task loss so it is trained as a full classifier, not a feature feeder — dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3698767) — but deep supervision alone does not stop two co-trained branches from *co-adapting into correlated errors*; the Hui–Walter conditional-independence constraint is the missing piece that forces them apart.
- **The analogy:** our ID channel and frozen-text channel are exactly **two imperfect diagnostic tests of the same latent quantity — "is item j the user's next choice?"** — on different, count-dependent reliabilities (ID credible only for warm items; text credible for all, including true-cold where ID has no gold standard at all). The double-dissociation IS the Hui–Walter regime: neither test is a gold standard, and their reliabilities cross over with observation count. The reason the additive stack / residual-head router fails cold is a **conditional-DEPENDENCE pathology**: trained additively, the text head learns only ID's residual, so its errors are maximally *correlated* with ID's — it is not a second independent test, it is ID's shadow, and stripping the ID row at eval leaves an uncalibrated shadow (3 hits, not 66). Hui–Walter prescribes the fix as a *training objective*: force the two channels' errors to be **conditionally independent given the target**, which is precisely what turns the text channel into a standalone test-form that scores cold items on its own (the sbert-only 66/153-hit ordering) while the ID channel keeps carrying warm CF.
- **Concrete co-training mechanism (pure-PyTorch, kept as a RESERVE spec — NOT grounded/queued):** a jointly-trained **dual-head** over the SAME encoder state `h` (NO second encoder — reuses `h`, cheap): head-ID scores `s_id = h @ E_id.T` (learned ID rows only), head-TXT scores `s_txt = h @ sbert_proj(sbert_table).T` (frozen-text projection only). Three loss terms, all default-OFF/zero-weight ⇒ current model bit-identical at init:
  1. **Deep supervision (standalone calibration):** add a full next-item CE on EACH head separately — `L_id = CE(s_id, y)` and `L_txt = CE(s_txt, y)` — so `sbert_proj` is trained as a *standalone metric* (the sbert-only regime that wins cold), not a residual. This is the piece the residual-head router lacked.
  2. **Hui–Walter conditional-independence penalty (the non-CS core):** penalize the correlation of the two heads' per-example *errors given the target*. Cheap realizable form: over each minibatch, take the two heads' target-logit residuals `r_id = s_id[y] − mean_j s_id[j]`, `r_txt = s_txt[y] − mean_j s_txt[j]` (or the two per-example CE losses), and add `λ · corr(r_id, r_txt)²` (batch Pearson correlation, one scalar) — driving the channels toward conditionally-independent errors so each is independently sufficient. `λ` zero-init ⇒ no-op; it is a *decorrelation regularizer at the loss*, not a fusion at the score.
  3. **Count-credibility inference (REUSE the already-built router):** at eval, merge with the EXISTING `Z=n/(n+k)` credibility weight (`--credibility-route` already in `run_sasrec_sbert.py`) — but now over TWO genuinely standalone, deep-supervised, decorrelated heads, so the `Z→0` cold limit lands on the *calibrated* text ordering (the thing that scored 66), not a residual shadow (that scored 3). The router's failure was upstream (no standalone txt metric); items 1–2 manufacture the metric the router assumed existed.
- **Why no CS person would default to this:** CS cold-start fusion co-trains two towers with a *shared/joint* objective and lets a learned gate or a dot-product mix them — which structurally ENCOURAGES conditional dependence (the towers co-adapt to minimize one joint loss; the weaker channel becomes a residual, exactly our failure). "Deliberately train two channels to be **conditionally-independent imperfect tests of a latent variable, each standalone-sufficient, then read observation-count as which test to trust**" is diagnostic-epidemiology / latent-class-measurement thinking (Hui–Walter, no-gold-standard test evaluation), not representation learning. My prior-art sweep (two-tower cold-start: ieee 10373857, arXiv:2108.13592; MoE-transformation cold-start; M²VAE arXiv:2508.00452) found the towers and the gates but **no CS cold-start work imposing a conditional-independence / error-decorrelation constraint to force each channel into a standalone-calibrated relevance test** — the searches explicitly returned "does not address conditional independence, decorrelation, or per-channel calibration."
- **Novelty vs ledger:** distinct from **CF1 cue-fusion** (additive inverse-variance fusion, DEAD — fuses at the representation in ONE space, never trains two standalone metrics), from **conn-gate / Z1 / X1** (all learnable single-space blends, DEAD), from the **credibility-router** (eval-time z-equate of a residual head, REFUTED — this FIXES its named upstream cause), and from the row-level imputation family (cold-synth/kriging/norm, EXHAUSTED — those rewrite the cold row, this changes the *training objective* so the text channel is independently calibrated). The novel axis is a **conditional-independence co-training constraint** — nothing in the ~60-probe ledger decorrelates two channels' errors given the target.
- **Feasibility / risk (as a reserve, if a supervisor ever re-opens co-training):** all three terms are zero-init/default-OFF no-ops ⇒ safe; cost is one extra catalog scoring (`h @ Ptext.T`, `Ptext` cacheable) + a batch-scalar correlation — cheap; leak-free (frozen text, train-only `item_freq`, no target consulted beyond the standard CE). **BUT it is unambiguously a MODEL-ARCHITECTURE / co-training change — squarely inside the CLOSED new-lever search — so it is NOT groundable now; it needs an EXPLICIT supervisor re-open of the co-training surface** (cycle-18: "a co-trained architecture is required"). Main risk if ever run: the decorrelation penalty could trade warm CF for cold calibration (the same tension) — but it is a *tunable* `λ`, and its predicted-outcome map is the honest headline either way (does forced conditional-independence let ONE model keep warm ~0.043 AND capture the standalone-text cold ordering, or is the tension irreducible even with two co-trained standalone heads? — the latter would be the *strongest possible* impossibility result, closing even the co-training door).
- **WHAT I GROUND THIS WINDOW: NOTHING (correct + directive-compliant).** The branch is terminally CLOSED per cycle-18; no cycle-19/pivot has fired. This block SUPERSEDES the vaguer "jointly-trained two-tower / MoE with count-gated hard route" reserve stub in XF 2026-07-03-2 with a *specific, prior-art-vetted, mechanistically-complete* co-training spec — so that the instant a supervisor (or the human) elects to re-open the co-training surface, in-field can ground a runnable proposal in one step instead of starting cold. It remains a RESERVE, explicitly UN-grounded.
- **FLAG FOR SUPERVISOR (cycle-19).** Terminal condition holds: the credibility-router REFUTED both variants; the row-imputation family is EXHAUSTED; the encoder-matched KRIGE-on-HSTU re-runs (494 JSON) corroborate closure. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED` (correct). Per cycle-18 the program reverts terminally to PAPER-FINISHING. The ONLY experimental surface the impossibility boundary leaves open is a **co-trained standalone-text-metric architecture**; if you elect to re-open it, the Hui–Walter conditional-independence dual-head above is the pre-vetted, mechanistically-ready entry. Absent that explicit re-open, the correct steady state is HOLD.
- **Reopening / next-idea condition:** a supervisor **cycle-19** (or the human) (i) explicitly re-opens the co-training surface, or (ii) sanctions a genuine PIVOT to a new benchmark/metric/contribution ⇒ next window grounds ONE idea (the Hui–Walter dual-head if (i)). Absent either: HOLD.
